+
+Record: implementation
+Category: enhancement
+Status: ready-for-agent
+Execution: claimed
+Assignee: root
+Blocked by: 22
+Validation: NOT_RUN
+Evidence: WORK/reports/japan-development-20261102/
+
+## Scope
+
+按日本方案修订2/3实施三国产业、独立发展、团结、政治与外交，保留原崩溃恢复及游戏数据。候选 WORK/exports/japan-development-20261102-a，父提交c4bbc194ddafcb2bb62f2bb89746c95f0b7b7f49。
+
+## Checkpoint
+
+已写入63文件差异，首轮检查问题已修部分；最终外部检查、文案终审、两轴审查和部署均未完成。没有启动游戏。最新作者制度/文风纠正为交付前置，详见任务22及WORK/reports/canon-prose-20261102/work-state-and-request.md。
+
+## Authorization
+
+用户允许全部更新、外部测试和文风审查完成后启动游戏，切入任意国家再观察者、五速运行留待用户。若启动失败/崩溃，按日志定向修复重试。此授权不表示目前可以跳过上述测试。

+

+Category: bug
+Status: ready-for-agent
+Execution: claimed
+Assignee: root
+Blocked by: none

+Evidence: WORK/reports/canon-prose-20261102/
+
+## Scope
+
+采用作者最新南北朝制度纠正、附件改稿和吴朝礼法注释；沿调用链审校中英事件、日志、按钮与状态。明确地方范围、行动主体、事件因果、提议/结果及政治路线。技能保存可复用纠正。保留已验证机制和用户原稿。
+
+独立候选 WORK/exports/canon-prose-20261102-a；日本候选单独保留。源头与工作账见本轮 work-state-and-request.md，逐文件原件见 entry-snapshot.json 指向的备份。
+
+## Acceptance
+
+全部纳入本轮的有效键有中英审校结论；附件文字与真实effect核对；覆盖层与调用键正确；新增动态引用按实际scope查证；机械文本与错误制度复核。必要源码接线做解析、Tiger差异和MCP查询。外部通过与游戏未运行分别报告。

  ce_cne_land_reform_remove: "Remove Jornal #bold Land Rights Revolution#!"
- ce_cne_land_reform_reason: "This movement originated in the Western Qin Great Revolution of 1810. People of this age viewed it as a modern economic transformation arising within agrarian eastern societies rooted in clan structures. Its method was to confiscate or redeem over-concentrated land from old owners and redistribute it among peasants, thereby freeing resources to the market. In politics, the reforms aim to grab power from old military nobles by physically reduce their wealth and means to control their 'clansmen'. As Northern Ming nobilities, with their highly militarised tradition in local ruling, are widely regarded as the source of chaos and poverty among the people of the old northern regimes. The Chinese imperial ruling bloc once suspended this transformation during its rise. But around Mu Zhongtang's 1820 southern campaign, to revive the economy, liberate production, and mobilize peasants and soldiers from the bottom, Mu and his confidants reopened the movement."
+ ce_cne_land_reform_reason: "This movement began with the Western Qin Great Revolution of 1810. People of the age understood it as a modern economic transformation arising among the agrarian states of the East, where powerful lineages had long dominated rural life. Through purchase or compulsory confiscation, land accumulated beyond all measure by its old owners was redistributed among a portion of the peasantry. The aim was to break the lineages’ hold over the rural population. The ruling group of the Empire of China suspended the movement during its rise to power. Around Mu Zhongtang’s southern campaign of 1820, however, he and his confidants revived it to restore the economy, free productive activity and mobilise peasants and soldiers."

+ ce_cne_land_reform:0 "Land Rights Revolution"
+ ce_cne_land_reform_reason:0 "This movement began with the Western Qin Great Revolution of 1810. People of the age understood it as a modern economic transformation arising among the agrarian states of the East, where powerful lineages had long dominated rural life. Through purchase or compulsory confiscation, land accumulated beyond all measure by its old owners was redistributed among a portion of the peasantry. The aim was to break the lineages’ hold over the rural population. The ruling group of the Empire of China suspended the movement during its rise to power. Around Mu Zhongtang’s southern campaign of 1820, however, he and his confidants revived it to restore the economy, free productive activity and mobilise peasants and soldiers."
